# Seed 33 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS (Iteration 112)

`seed 33 tag2 8h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 10 --cpus 2,8`,
commit 51d21aa7d (Iteration 111), started 04:41: four workers (cpus 12, 2, 8,
4), population 256, 141 generations before the deadline, 35,862 designs;
2,061 refused by the reach limit, no other death. Session 29 measured it with
seeds 27, 28, 30 and 32 (calibration 0.996; cpu 2 0.996, cpu 8 0.987) - 520
designs, 2 h 18 min: the fronts have grown since sizes are honest.

**The stopping rule: small** - the first since seed 32.

| objective | seed 33's best | before (seeds 27-32, this session) | improvement | bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| fastest | 0.132 (caeb3d97ac) | 0.133 (seed 32) | +0.8% | 5% |
| smallest binary + image | 22,254 (ffe6038baf) | 22,342 (seed 32) | +0.39% | 2% |
| least memory | 188 KB | 188 KB | none | 8 KB |

The front of all runs: **138** - 102 of seed 33, 33 of seed 32, one each of
27, 28 and 30; 48 by memory alone. The fastest caeb3d97ac 0.132 at 37,928
bytes; the smallest ffe6038baf 22,254 (1.272). Q (information): log-range
best 6d3f8eac19 (0.308 at 24,971 bytes, 192 KB); plain product best
85cd449e82 (0.148 at 34,160). The small end crept the whole run: 22,342 at
the start, 22,286 by generation 100, 22,254 at the end.

On the front of all runs: opbody 130, t2wide 113, hotinl 96, the JIT 33,
rtempty 25, jitx 10.

## The run's report

# Evolved VM designs

35862 designs evaluated, 33801 alive. Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, parse, corpus, loop,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
